## Epistemic Propagation Chains

### Root #1 (`trade_offered` @ tick 0): 周烬(刻刀/白面) offers 2 nutrient_ration (tier 1) for 2 coins
  - tick 1 `trade_rejected` (`bai_ya` -> `zhou_jin`): 白鸦(暗网中枢) rejected offer
